## Go-live checklist

Actions that are deliberately deferred during the build and must happen at (or just before) publish — not before, because doing them early would affect the live Flux site or the live store.

**Before checking any of the eight pages below off as broken: try `?view=<suffix>` first.** Shop All, the four occasion collections (Slow Mornings, Entertaining, Sunday Roast, High Tea), Cafe, Our Story, and Contact cannot have their real template assigned in Admin until Horizon is published — the Theme template dropdown only lists templates belonging to the *currently published* theme (Flux), so none of these eight even appear as options today. Until then, a bare URL (`/pages/cafe`, `/collections/all`, …) on the preview theme renders Horizon's generic default template instead — no error, nothing visibly broken, just the wrong page. That is expected, not a regression. To see the real, built page, add the template suffix explicitly: `?preview_theme_id=182395437357&view=cafe`, `?view=all`, `?view=occasion`, `?view=our-story`, `?view=contact`, etc. This has already produced two false "the page is broken" alarms during verification — check with `?view=` before concluding something regressed.

- [ ] **Primary navigation: point the header at the real menu, one way or the other.** `main-menu` is a store-level object shared with the live Flux site — editing it now would change Flux's navigation immediately, so the build uses a separate menu instead (`sections/header-group.json`'s `_header-menu` block, `"menu"` setting — confirmed this is a plain `link_list` setting, no core edit needed to point it at any handle). At publish time, do one of:
  - **(a) Switch the header's `"menu"` setting to `"main-menu"`** once `main-menu` itself has been updated in Admin to match the design's navigation (see the proposal in conversation — Shop / Wholesale / Cafe / Our Story / Contact), or
  - **(b) Update `main-menu` directly to match the design**, then no header setting change is needed at all — it already defaults to `"main-menu"`.

  Whichever path, confirm the live nav matches the design before publish, not after — this is easy to forget since the draft theme will look correct throughout the build regardless of which menu `main-menu` itself contains.
- [ ] Reconcile `redirects.md` against `url-baseline.md` and confirm every changed URL has a redirect in the admin (already tracked in `CLAUDE.md`, restated here for one checklist to work from).
- [x] **Cafe page map: resolved 2026-08-26 — client provided a real Google Maps embed iframe, now live.** `map_placeholder` in `templates/page.cafe.json` no longer holds the placeholder box (pin icon, address, "Google Maps embed" label) — it's a `custom-liquid` block rendering the client's iframe at the exact same footprint (border `1px solid #E3D6C5`, `border-radius: 6px`, background `#F4F1EA`, same position/width as the box it replaced), responsive via `aspect-ratio: 4/3` rather than Google's fixed 600×450px, `loading="lazy"`, and a `title` for screen readers. Verified live via `?view=cafe` on both desktop (547×410px, matching the "Reserve your table" card's height) and mobile (343×257px, full column width) — interactive, "Open in Maps" control present. No CSP/`X-Frame-Options` issue: Horizon's response only sets `frame-ancestors 'none'` (governs who can frame the page, not what it can embed). **Found a real layout bug along the way — initially patched locally, then fixed at the source once it recurred.** `blocks/custom-liquid.liquid` wraps its output in a bare, class-less `<div>` with no width styling at all. Inside a column flex container with `align-items: flex-start` (the default cross-axis behaviour whenever a group's `horizontal_alignment_flex_direction_column` isn't `stretch`), that bare div has nothing establishing a real width — a percentage `width: 100%` on content inside it resolves against an already-indeterminate ancestor and collapses toward zero. First hit here, fixed with a scoped `<style>`/`:has()` override local to this one block. When the same root cause caused the same collapse on the Cafe booking buttons (a *second* instance, independently patched by dropping a forced `flex:1` split — see the button-wrap-fix commit), the two one-off patches were consolidated into a single source fix instead — see §6.
- [ ] **Page template assignments — `page.wholesale.json`, `page.our-story.json`, and `page.contact.json`, not yet assigned in Admin.**

  **Correction, 2026-08-20 — this was wrongly marked "safe to do now" and isn't.** `template_suffix` is a property of the *resource* (the page), not of a theme — it's shared across every theme on the store, including whichever one is currently published. Assigning it while working in Horizon's theme editor still writes a value the *live* Flux site reads too: if Flux has its own template matching that suffix name, the live page switches to it immediately; if the resource is currently on a real, in-use Flux template and the new suffix matches nothing in Flux, Flux falls back to its bare default and the live page's actual content disappears.

  - `page.wholesale.json` → `/pages/wholesale`. Live Flux renders `image_banner` + two `image_with_text` sections — a real, bespoke layout. **Not safe today.** Wait for go-live.
  - `page.our-story.json` → `/pages/lambruk-pantry-about-us`. Live Flux renders a slider, rich text, image-with-text, image gallery and a collection list — a real, bespoke layout. **Not safe today.** Wait for go-live.
  - `page.cafe.json` → the Cafe page. Live Flux content is just the word "Cafe," one generic section, nothing else. Likely safe, but still check the "Theme template" dropdown in Admin before assigning, don't assume from this alone.
  - `page.contact.json` → `/pages/contact`. Live Flux content not checked from the theme side (no Admin access) — same caution as the other two: check the "Theme template" dropdown and Flux's actual rendered content before assigning, don't assume safety from this note alone.

- [ ] **Collection template assignments — five, all confirmed genuinely impossible before publish, not just deferred out of caution.** Checked directly (2026-08-25): the Theme template dropdown in Admin only ever lists templates that exist in the currently *published* theme. Horizon (`182395437357`) isn't published — Flux is — so none of the five templates below appear as options at all today, regardless of how ready or safe any of them are. This isn't the same caution as the page templates above (which *could* technically be assigned now but would affect Flux); these literally cannot be selected in Admin until Horizon is the published theme. Confirmed for `collection.all.json` specifically: the dropdown offered only "Default collection" and "sale," both from Flux.

  Assign all five **immediately after publishing Horizon, before doing anything else** — go to each collection in Admin and set:

  | Collection | Theme template to select |
  |---|---|
  | Shop All | `all` |
  | Slow Mornings | `occasion` |
  | Entertaining | `occasion` |
  | Sunday Roast | `occasion` |
  | High Tea | `occasion` |

  **A missed assignment fails silently — there is no error, broken link, or visibly missing content.** The collection just renders as an ordinary collection page (Horizon's bare default grid: plain title, no eyebrow, no curated header, no occasion hero image, none of this build's actual design) instead of the intended one. It still works, still shows products, still looks like a real page — nothing flags that the wrong template is in use, which is exactly what made `collection.all.json` invisible for this entire build (§36/§37) until it was checked directly. Between publish and these five assignments, all five pages are live on the real storefront rendering wrong — treat this as done in the same sitting as publishing, not as a followup task.
